[SEC_BLOCKED] 2026-11-24 | 86e2785 32126521+Vimurai@users.noreply.github.com | E-473 at 86e2785: 7 EXPLOITED rows, one root cause. The tests/run.sh ~/.rein guard checks mtime (find -P -type f -newer), so writes made by rsync -a (the installer's own copy form), cp -p and touch -d are not seen, nor are renames, chmods, new symlinks or a symlinked ~/.rein. The run then prints "untouched". Fix verified in the harness: find -H ... \( -type f -o -type l \) -cnewer stamp, plus a harness_isolation row that writes with rsync -a. P3 notes, not gating: REIN_TEST_GUARD_HOME is honoured from any env; the operator's live sessions can falsely fail `rein ci run`. Cleared: hook traps (new == old exit codes), mcp-result (args and errors pass through), legacy globs (basename-only, prompt class, symlink report-only).
